## Modules

- [0001_wave0_probe](modules/0001_wave0_probe.md) - Create the Wave 0 PostgreSQL lifecycle probe.
- [20260928_0001_initial_schema](modules/20260928_0001_initial_schema.md) - Create the complete initial WorkChord schema.
- [20260930_0002_profile_capacity](modules/20260930_0002_profile_capacity.md) - Add durable profile availability without changing the frozen initial schema.
- [20260930_0003_delivery_dependencies](modules/20260930_0003_delivery_dependencies.md) - Persist delivery dependencies separately from local schedule edges.
- [20260930_0004_discussion](modules/20260930_0004_discussion.md) - Add retained discussion, subscriptions and the personal inbox transport.
- [20261003_0005_native_connections](modules/20261003_0005_native_connections.md) - Add short-lived browser-approved native connections.
- [20261004_0006_delivery_observations](modules/20261004_0006_delivery_observations.md) - Retain event-time delivery observations without guessing older history.
- [20261004_0007_execution_usage](modules/20261004_0007_execution_usage.md) - Retain execution usage revisions and immutable pricing snapshots.
- [20261007_0008_time_entries](modules/20261007_0008_time_entries.md) - Retain private minute records and append-only correction history.
- [20261008_0009_project_identity](modules/20261008_0009_project_identity.md) - Preserve project allocation identity across retained history.
- [20261009_0010_allocation_identity](modules/20261009_0010_allocation_identity.md) - Preserve allocation lifetimes across recovery and deletion.
- [20261009_0011_profile_identity](modules/20261009_0011_profile_identity.md) - Preserve profile lifetimes across recovery and deletion.
- [20261111_0012_session_work_profile](modules/20261111_0012_session_work_profile.md) - Let a trusted-local browser session choose the person profile it works as.
- [20261111_0013_local_comment_authors](modules/20261111_0013_local_comment_authors.md) - Let a trusted-local session comment as the person profile it works as.
- [20261115_0014_observer_sessions](modules/20261115_0014_observer_sessions.md) - Record a session's access level, and what a pairing request asks for.
- [20261116_0015_agdash_link_provider](modules/20261116_0015_agdash_link_provider.md) - Accept agdash session links as an external link provider.
- [20261116_0016_runtime_observations](modules/20261116_0016_runtime_observations.md) - Retain advisory observations of agent runtime sessions.
- [20261116_0017_run_harness_session](modules/20261116_0017_run_harness_session.md) - Keep a run's declared harness session in columns a reader can search.
- [20261120_0018_review_provenance](modules/20261120_0018_review_provenance.md) - Preserve execution provenance and project review selection.
- [20261121_0019_execution_planning_window](modules/20261121_0019_execution_planning_window.md) - Retain server-proved execution equivalence across a planning reservation.
- [20261121_0020_annual_calendar_coverage](modules/20261121_0020_annual_calendar_coverage.md) - Store explicitly selected annual calendars for iterations and profile owners.
- [20261124_0021_maintenance_windows](modules/20261124_0021_maintenance_windows.md) - Record coordinated maintenance windows and the receipts that admit them.
- [20261124_0022_capacity_adjustments](modules/20261124_0022_capacity_adjustments.md) - Record dated availability adjustments on iteration capacity rows.
- [20261124_0023_task_replacements](modules/20261124_0023_task_replacements.md) - Record audited clean-replacement dispositions between tasks.
- [20261124_0024_delivery_dependency_target_branch](modules/20261124_0024_delivery_dependency_target_branch.md) - Let a delivery prerequisite require accepted code merged on a named branch.
- [20261126_0025_catalog_local_first](modules/20261126_0025_catalog_local_first.md) - Let a model catalog entry prefer a free or local model at equal adequacy.
- [20261126_0026_review_record_kind](modules/20261126_0026_review_record_kind.md) - Tell an owner's override acceptance apart from an ordinary review in review history.
- [20261126_0027_task_postponements](modules/20261126_0027_task_postponements.md) - Record postponed tasks and the requirements that must hold before they resume.
- [20261126_0028_team_runtime_identity](modules/20261126_0028_team_runtime_identity.md) - Record which runtime instance acknowledged each team member's handoff.
- [20261126_0029_catalog_effort](modules/20261126_0029_catalog_effort.md) - Record the reasoning-effort setting of each model catalog entry.

- [single-page-toolbar](modules/single-page-toolbar.md) - /** * A page has at most one toolbar row. For every value a function returns, this rule works out * which `PageToolbar` elements can be on screen together and reports every one after the first. * * Which component is the toolbar is decided by its binding, not by the name written in JSX: * a named import with or without an alias, a default import, a namespace import (`ui.PageToolbar`), * a name the imported local module re-exports for it, and constants or wrappers that hold it. * * Where a toolbar element can be held, the rule follows it: JSX trees and fragments, attributes and * render props, variables (every assignment counts, because the rule does not follow control flow), * destructuring, object properties, array elements, values added later (`rows.a = …`, `push`), * local helper functions and their return values, local components rendered as JSX, and callbacks * passed to calls (a `map` or `flatMap` callback renders once per item, so its toolbar counts twice). * * Only two things are accepted as exclusive: the two arms of one conditional expression, and * separate return statements. One member read with a computed key (`views[mode]`) reads one value, * so it counts like a conditional over the values it can read. Everything else that the rule cannot * prove exclusive is reported, and the message says so. * * Toolbars inside a component imported from another file are not seen here; the page-chrome check * counts them in the rendered page. */
